@@125320710 <h> OPINION <h> The JDI , Hamas &amp; genocide : A reply to Lawrence Nowosenetz <p> Mervyn Bennun <p> 29 May 2025 <p> Mervyn Bennun says parallels between Zionism and apartheid are blatant <p> The concept of a " race " is a creature of the social sciences <p> As one of the allegedly " self-hating " Jews , being a member of the South African Jews for a Free Palestine ( SAJFP ) referred to by Lawrence Nowosenetz in Politicsweb , may I have leave to reply to his piece ? <p> Much of Nowosenetz 's article centres around a meeting arranged by the Jewish Democratic Initiative ( JDI ) . Its purpose was to have a frank discussion on whether Israeli actions in Gaza in response to the events of 7 October 2023 constitute genocide , as alleged in the International Court of Justice ( ICJ ) . <p> It was made clear to those attending that , by being present , each had agreed to proceed under the so-called Chatham House Rules . As the JDI explained , this means that the participants agreed that the proceedings @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ present would not be quoted . <p> Nowosenetz was not there , but someone who had been disclosed to him what purports to be its proceedings . Nowosenetz decided for himself that the Chatham House Rules were " inappropriate " in order to do precisely what the JDI explained what it wished to avoid -- " right-wing trolling and smear tactics " . The JDI issued its " sincere apologies to those members of the audience whose contributions , made in good faith , were quoted out of context , and whose personal integrity was insulted " . <p> The incident is unsavoury and has consequences . Nowosenetz is a lawyer for whom confidentiality is apparently a matter for ad hoc and opportunist application . Hopefully , JDI will publicly identify his informant so that they can both be excluded in future when participants are invited to speak in confidence . Neither of them has anything worthwhile to contribute , for the Chatham House Rules are designed to get the best out of people whose thoughts are worth listening to . <p> --&gt; <p> In his first paragraph Nowosenetz sets @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ . He is clear : those who do n't , support Hamas . <p> I have in front of me as I write this my old green South African identity card . Issued in April 1956 and slightly larger than today 's ' plastic ' , it has a photograph of twenty years ' old me , my surname and initials , and records me as South African Citizen 331 384468 W. The " W " was crucial -- it denotes that I am a member of the ' White ' race . <p> The concept of a " race " is a creature of the social sciences . This was demonstrated by the gibberish of the Population Registration Act 1950 , which stated that a member of the " white " race was someone who -- ' <p> " in appearance obviously is , or who is generally accepted as a white person , but does not include a person who , although in appearance obviously a white person , is generally accepted as a coloured person . " <p> --&gt; <p> Opposition to apartheid was not tolerated , @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ Over the years the consequences of opposition to apartheid grew increasingly harsh . <p> Whether Israel likes it or not , the International Court of Justice has ruled after an exhaustive examination of the law and Israel 's conduct that Israel is in violation of the International Convention on the Elimination of All Forms of Racial Discrimination -- bluntly , that Israel is an apartheid state . This is in fact exactly what Zionism has as its objective . <p> Just as South Africa accorded whites alone citizen status , so Israel 's Basic Law provides that Israel is the nation state of the Jewish People " in which it realizes its natural , cultural , religious and historical right to self-determination , " and that the right to national self-determination in the State of Israel " is exclusive to the Jewish People " . <p> If one assumes that there is a " Jewish People " or race or ethnicity , parallel to the South African illusion of a white race or people or nation , then those who challenge this image of Jews can readily be defined by @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ of pogroms , expulsions , migrations , conversions , emigration , immigration , mixed relationships and all the rest of what has happened , then the phrase " Jewish People " as used by Zionism is racist , and racism is a social construction -- like being " white " . <p> --&gt; <p> In fact , published research has established that Jews of Middle Eastern origin and Palestinians are genetically indistinguishable . The research concluded that rivalry between Palestinians and Jews " is based in cultural and religious , but not in genetic differences " . This provoked such a uproar by Zionists that the journal 's publishers ordered libraries to physically tear the text from the issue . Fortunately , the work has been preserved . The Science editor of the Guardian commented -- <p> " Such a drastic act of self-censorship is unprecedented in research publishing and has created widespread disquiet , generating fears that it may involve the suppression of scientific work that questions Biblical dogma " . <p> The editor quotes a scientist who objected to the censorship and said that if its author -- @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ genetically very special , instead of ordinary , you can be sure no one would have objected to the phrases he used in his article . This is a very sad business . " <p> --&gt; <p> There are people in the world who are Jews , but there is no " Jewish People " in the sense that Zionist Israel 's Basic Law uses the term . The Nazis tried to invent an " Aryan " ethnicity and apartheid South Africa tried to invent a " white " one ; history 's grim record was the result in both cases . <p> Nothing makes this clearer than Tony Greenstein 's Zionism During the Holocaust . He describes how comfortable Zionists were with the " white " ethnic myth of apartheid and , initially , with the Nazi 's " Aryan " myth . In fact , Zionists initially welcomed Hitler 's rise to power . <p> The history of Jews is simply the history of the countries where they live or lived , and that accords us no less dignity or worth . <p> So far as the atrocities @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ has from the outset , and on several occasions subsequently , condemned the harm done to Israeli civilians and the hostage-taking by Hamas and other Palestinian armed groups . This condemnation is repeated in the first paragraph of the South African Application to the ICJ under the Convention on the Prevention and Punishment of the Crime of Genocide ( briefly , the Genocide Convention ) . <p> The crime of genocide is defined in two components by Article II of the Genocide Convention in a manner familiar to lawyers . The first component describes the prohibited physical act or state of affairs , and the second describes the state of mind of those who brought about what is prohibited and who are therefore guilty . There is much legal theory embedded in this , but it is summarised in two Latin phrases widely familiar to lawyers and used in the ICJ : the first component is the actus reus and the second is the mens rea . <p> The actus reus of genocide is stated to be " any of the following acts " against " a national , ethnical @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ <p> " ( a ) Killing members of the group ; <p> " ( b ) Causing serious bodily or mental harm to members of the group ; <p> " ( c ) Deliberately inflicting on the group conditions of life calculated to bring about its <p> physical destruction in whole or in part ; <p> " ( d ) Imposing measures intended to prevent births within the group ; <p> " ( e ) Forcibly transferring children of the group to another group " . <p> The mens rea is stated to be the " intent to destroy " the group " in whole or in part " . <p> The ICJ can hear only cases involving states . Whether the individuals involved in the events of 7 October 2023 committed genocide or not is therefore a matter for the International Criminal Court ( ICC ) to determine . Article III of the Genocide Convention ensures that individuals may be prosecuted for genocide , conspiracy to commit genocide , direct and public incitement to commit genocide , an attempt to commit genocide , and complicity in genocide . <p> @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ of the other acts enumerated in Article III shall be punished , whether they are constitutionally responsible rulers , public officials or private individuals " . It appears that the Palestinians against whom the ICC issued warrants of arrest in respect of 7 October 2023 have been targeted and assassinated by Israel . <p> It is inappropriate to comment on what participants at the JDI meeting are said to have said . Nowosenetz himself , however , concludes his blog by claiming that " the evidence clearly points to the real genocide enterprise being uniquely by Hamas " , referring to 7 October 2023 . He reaches this conclusion without reference to evidence in the case placed before the ICJ by South Africa . <p> This evidence is contained in the Application . On 27 February 2025 it was It was supplemented by a document entitled " Public dossier of openly available evidence on the State of Israel 's acts of genocide against the Palestinians in Gaza , as of 4 February 2025 " . This has been deposited with the United Nations Security Council , as required by the @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ phrase prima facie ( idiomatically , " on the face of it " ) the ICJ used the word " plausibly " . It approached the case before it methodically ; after settling preliminary and procedural matters , it then examined the evidence in South Africa 's Application . <p> It considered first the actus reus . It found that it is plausible that Palestinians have rights protected by the Genocide Convention , and that it was plausible that there is a risk of irreparable prejudice to these rights , and that the matter was urgent . It did this by examining the evidence of the conditions created in Gaza . Each piece of evidence is supported by a verifiable reference in a footnote . <p> It then determined that the mens rea had been plausibly established by examining statements by senior Israeli officials of what Israel intended to do . Again , each statement is explicitly supported by a verifiable footnote -- they number from 439-519 . <p> It is important to note that the truth of none of the evidence has been challenged by Israel . What its @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ addition to noting the quality of the evidence from organisations such as the World Health Organisation , Save the Children , Unicef , and Oxfam , when referring to the evidence from organs of the United Nations Norman Finkelstein described the strategy used by South Africa as " very clever " -- <p> " Because you could n't seriously discount the evidence without simultaneously discounting the whole United Nations system , because their whole case was built on the UN system . And no judge sitting on the highest judicial body of the UN could very well come along and credibly argue that all the UN organisations are lying , that all the UN organisations are antisemitic , that all the UN organisations are engaged in a concerted conspiracy against Israel " . <p> The ICJ summarised its provisional decision by stating it considered that -- <p> " there is urgency , in the sense that there is a real and imminent risk that irreparable prejudice will be caused to the rights found by the Court to be plausible , before it gives its final decision " . <p> Popularly @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ plausibly committing genocide . Israel has rejected this vehemently , describing it as antisemitic . <p> The adjective " antisemitic " may describe Zionist Israel 's perception of the decision but , as the evidence was unchallenged , it casts no light on where the ICJ 's reasoning is thought to have been wrong . Israel filed no papers , depending on its Counsel 's oral arguments . <p> The ICJ noted that Israel emphasised -- <p> " that it bears the responsibility to protect its citizens , including those captured and held hostage as a result of the attack that took place on 7 October 2023 . As a consequence , it claims that its right to self-defence is critical to any evaluation of the present situation . " <p> The ICJ made no further reference to self-defence . Its provisional findings are probably a hint that it had considered that the limits of self-defence had been exceeded . <p> The real basis of Israel 's defence is to be found in the address by Israel 's leading Counsel , Professor Malcolm Shaw . South Africa based its case @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ the hands of Zionism ; Professor Shaw , however , denied the relevance of anything preceding the events of 7 October 2023 , saying that " such acts may be seen as the real genocide in this situation " . <p> For the context to be irrelevant , the Palestinians must have no history . Granting Palestinians a history would deny one of Zionism 's basic tenets . Article II of the Genocide Convention protects only a " national , ethnical , racial or religious group " and , by finding that Palestinians are such a group and therefore have a history the ICJ is , in Zionist eyes , antisemitic . <p> South Africa filed its papers on 28 October 2024 in preparation for the final hearing before the ICJ . Israel was to have filed its reply on 28 July 2025 , but applied for and was granted an extension till 12 January 2026 . <p> And this takes me back to my old green South African identity card and its " W " . <p> In 1964 , I was a young Advocate and was briefed in @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ people who were being prosecuted for allegedly being members of the banned African National Congress . There were many such trials , and the Report of the Truth and Reconciliation Commission describes some of what happened . I was inexperienced , and the Special Branch did their best to intimidate me . <p> Apartheid was a doctrine enforced by Prime Minister Hendrik Verwoerd in South Africa , but he recognised it in Israel . In 1961 he said , " Israel had been taken away from the Arabs after the Arabs lived there for a thousand years . .... Israel , like South Africa , is an apartheid state " . His successor , J.B. Vorster , was welcomed in Israel and laid a wreath at Yad Vashem in memory of the victims whose annihilation he had supported with such commitment as a Nazi that he had been detained during World War Two . <p> Anti-apartheid whites were not popular and Jews , being disproportionately represented in the anti-apartheid struggle , were especially disliked . The parallels between Zionism and apartheid are blatant , and Nowosenetz does a good job @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ people like du Preez , being called a " self-hating Jew " by Nowosenetz because I 'm anti-Zionist is small beer .

@@125343018 <h> A movement against silencing : What the war in Palestine has taught us about journalism <p> One of the most revealing takeaways from the genocide in Gaza has been the profound threat posed to journalism . Even in this era of artificial intelligence and disinformation , truth remains a powerful force . And the most brutal way to silence truth is to eliminate those who report it . <p> This has been the clear strategy adopted by Israel . A recent study found that more journalists have been killed since 7 October 2023 than in any other conflict in modern history -- more than the total deaths combined in the U.S Civil War , World War I , World War II , The Korean War , Vietnam , Yugoslavia , and post 9/11 Afghanistan . <p> A Lancetreport estimated that Palestinian deaths are 40% higher than figures provided by the Palestinian Ministry of Health . At the time of writing , the official toll stood at 54,056 but the actual number may be closer to 100,000 . Similarly , the number of journalists killed continues to climb , reported @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ several more targeted attacks since , hence we can estimate that death toll to be 250 by May 2025 . In all likelihood , the actual figure is far higher . <h> On the Ground in South Africa <p> In 2022 , I was approached by the Salt River Heritage Society in Cape Town to speak about the murals they had commissioned in their neighbourhood . Central among them was one depicting Shireen Abu Akleh , the Al Jazeera journalist assassinated by an Israeli sniper on 11 May 2022 . Initially , Israeli authorities falsely blamed Palestinian militants . By the time her murder was verified , the media cycle had moved on . A full year later , on 12 May 2023 , the Israeli Defence Force issued a hollow apology . Shireen 's killing epitomised a strategy of " killing the truth " -- the deliberate targeting of journalists , a war crime under international humanitarian law . <p> Cape Town , more than any city in Southern Africa , has consistently demonstrated loud and visible support for Palestine . Since the genocide began , countless events -- @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ exhibitions , concerts , interfaith gatherings , and vigils -- have been held . <p> Just two weeks into the onslaught , on 22 October 2023 , I was invited by the Palestinian Solidarity Committee ( PSC ) to speak at the first major protest for Gaza , again in Salt River , specifically to address the killing of journalists . At that point , 17 journalists had already been murdered . No one could have predicted the escalation that followed . <p> Since then , my commitment to this issue has only deepened . As an independent journalist and freelancer , I have had the privilege of speaking freely -- at protests , on radio , television , and social media -- during a time when many employed journalists feared for their jobs . I 've used my voice with the hope that it might echo enough to spark accountability . So far , that hope remains unfulfilled . <p> Yet despite over 600 days of relentless bombardment and destruction , young journalists in Gaza persist . One of the most remarkable examples is nine-year-old Lama Jamous , who @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ of her neighbourhood . <p> Perhaps the most meaningful solidarity effort in South Africa was the organisation of national vigils on 28 January 2024 . Held in Cape Town , Johannesburg , KwaZulu-Natal , and Makhanda , these gatherings condemned the systematic murder of Palestinian journalists . Journalists across the country united to honour their colleagues abroad . In Cape Town , award-winning journalist Zubeida Jaffer spoke movingly about her experiences reporting during apartheid and covering the Rwandan genocide -- drawing clear parallels with Gaza . Many veteran journalists agreed : the conditions Palestinians face today are even more brutal than those under South African apartheid . <p> From these vigils emerged a WhatsApp group called Journalists Against Apartheid , a platform for solidarity , awareness , and resistance among South African media workers . <h> A Divided Media <p> The genocide in Gaza has unmasked the stark divide in global journalism . Palestinian journalists have redefined what it means to do this work . Their commitment is n't driven by money or recognition -- it is a moral imperative . Despite losing homes , loved ones , and access @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ carried injured children into hospitals , buried colleagues , and dug survivors from rubble -- all while documenting the unfolding horror . They appear on our screens , exhausted yet unwavering , embodying what it means to serve truth . <p> In contrast , Western media has disgraced itself . It has become a factory of bias , Islamophobia , and propaganda . One of the most damaging cases emerged on 14 October 2023 -- the viral lie that Hamas had beheaded 40 babies . First shared by Israeli soldiers , the claim was repeated by then-U.S . President Joe Biden without evidence . The result ? A white supremacist in Chicago murdered six-year-old Palestinian-American Wadea Alfayoumi in a hate crime -- stabbing him 26 times . Only in May 2025 was the perpetrator charged . To date , Israel has not retracted or apologised for the lie that sparked the killing . <p> This misinformation continues . False claims about " white genocide " in South Africa have also gained traction in U.S. right-wing media circles -- echoing Trump-era tactics of distraction and demonisation . <p> Western journalists have @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ MSNBC fired journalists for supporting Palestine . Outlets like CNN and The New York Times led with fake headlines and unverified stories -- many of which were later retracted , but not before irreparable harm was done . <p> " A claim is circulated without evidence ; Western journalists spread it like wildfire ; diplomats and politicians parrot it ; a narrative is built ; the general public believes it -- and the damage is done . " <p> In response , citizen journalism has risen powerfully . Platforms like Instagram and TikTok have become vehicles for truth , elevating voices on the ground . Ordinary people , wearing " PRESS " vests , risk their lives to document reality . The use of mobile phones makes Israeli atrocities harder to conceal -- though the regime has responded with censorship , shadowbanning , and algorithmic suppression . <h> A Commitment to Truth <p> In October 2024 , Al Jazeera correspondent Youmna El Sayed visited South Africa . Hearing her firsthand account was life-changing . Younger than me , yet infinitely more brave , she described war from the perspective of @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ Bo-Kaap , she recounted being given five minutes by Israeli forces to evacuate her home with her husband and four children . <p> " My 8-year-old daughter Sireen 's biggest fear was surviving alone , " she said . " Every night she asked us to sleep close together and said , ' Mom , if a rocket hits , let it kill us all so no one is left behind . ' " <p> El Sayed spoke of displacement , the stench of decaying bodies , and the total dehumanisation they endured . <p> " Journalists in Gaza were targeted everywhere : in our homes , in the field , in our cars -- with no mercy and no regard for humanitarian laws . " <p> " Israel barred international journalists from entering Gaza , hoping to hide its crimes . But it underestimated the resilience of Palestinian journalists -- continuing to work with no food , no water , and bombed-out offices . " <p> We all remember the moment veteran journalist Wael Al-Dahdouh cradled the lifeless body of his son Hamza -- also a journalist -- killed @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ , who wrote a letter before his death in April : <p> The death toll among journalists will rise , as the genocide continues . But rather than deterring us , these deaths strengthen our resolve . We remain committed to ethical journalism , to truth-telling , and to bearing witness to injustice . <p> We will not be silenced . We owe that to those who gave their lives so the world could see . <p> *This article was first published in Media Review Network on 28 May 2025 <p> Atiyyah Khan is a journalist , activist , cultural worker and archivist . For the past 17 years , she has documented the arts in South Africa . Common themes in her work focus on topics such as spatial injustice , untold stories of apartheid , jazz history and underground art movements .
